ENTRY:
0000:F3                         DI
0001:AF                         XOR   A
0002:32 6800                    LD    (6800H),A         ; [6800] {00}
0005:C3 0674                    JP    start1            ; 0674

RST_08:
0008:C3 7800                    JP    VEC_RST_08        ; 7800

000B:E1                         POP   HL
000C:E9                         JP    (HL)              ; 7800

000D:00                         NOP
000E:00                         NOP
000F:00                         NOP

RST_10:
0010:C3 7803                    JP    VEC_RST_10        ; 7803

;******  DCB input (AF,DE)
;  I: DE = DCB address
;  O: A  = byte read
INCH:
0013:C5                         PUSH  BC
0014:06 01                      LD    B,1
0016:18 2E                      JR    DCB_in_out        ; $+46 0046

RST_18:
0018:C3 7806                    JP    VEC_RST_18        ; 7806

;******  DCB output (AF,DE)
;  I: DE = DCB address
;  I: A  = byte to output
;  O: -
DCB_output:
001B:C5                         PUSH  BC
001C:06 02                      LD    B,2
001E:18 26                      JR    DCB_in_out        ; $+38 0046

RST_20:
0020:C3 7809                    JP    VEC_RST_20        ; 7809

;******  DCB in/out (AF,DE)
;  I: DE = DCB address
;  O: ?
DCB_in_out:
0023:C5                         PUSH  BC
0024:06 04                      LD    B,4
0026:18 1E                      JR    DCB_in_out        ; $+30 0046

RST_28:
0028:C3 780C                    JP    VEC_RST_28        ; 780C

;******  get keyboard (AF,DE)
;  I: -
;  O: A = ASCII code of character
KEY_get:
002B:11 7815                    LD    DE,DCB_kbd_type
002E:18 E3                      JR    INCH              ; $-29 0013

RST_30:
0030:C3 780F                    JP    VEC_RST_30        ; 780F

;******  display (AF,DE)
;  print a character on the screen
;  I: A = ASCII code of character
;  O: -
OUTCH:
0033:11 781D                    LD    DE,DCB_scr_type
0036:18 E3                      JR    DCB_output        ; $-29 001B

RST_38:
0038:C3 2EB8                    JP    2EB8H             ; 2EB8

;******  print (AF,DE)
;  I: ASCII code of character to print
;  O: -
DCB_print:
003B:11 7825                    LD    DE,DCB_prt_type
003E:18 DB                      JR    DCB_output        ; $-37 001B

INLINE:
0040:C3 2EFD                    JP    2EFDH             ; 2EFD

0043:C9                         RET

0044:00                         NOP
0045:00                         NOP

DCB_in_out:
0046:C3 03C2                    JP    DCB_in_out_1      ; 03C2

;******  wait for key (AF,DE)
;  I: -
;  O: A = ASCII code of character
INCHW:
0049:CD 002B                    CALL  KEY_get           ; 002B
004C:B7                         OR    A
004D:C0                         RET   NZ
004E:18 F9                      JR    INCHW             ; $-7 0049

0050:2A 7820                    LD    HL,(DCB_scr_pos)  ; [7820] {ENTRY}
0053:7E                         LD    A,(HL)            ; [0000] {F3}
0054:32 783C                    LD    (KEY_old_6),A     ; [783C] {00}
0057:C9                         RET

0058:4C                         LD    C,H
0059:FE 54                      CP    "T"
005B:20 D6                      JR    NZ,OUTCH          ; $-42 0033
005D:FD 21 0BF1                 LD    IY,0BF1H
0061:78                         LD    A,B
0062:B1                         OR    C
0063:20 FB                      JR    NZ,sleep_BC       ; $-5 0060
0065:C9                         RET

;******  NMI entry (reset key)
reset:
0066:31 0600                    LD    SP,0600H
0069:3A 68EC                    LD    A,(68ECH)         ; [68EC] {00}

;******  prepare ram for BASIC
start4:
006C:3C                         INC   A
006D:FE 02                      CP    2
006F:D2 0000                    JP    NC,ENTRY          ; 0000
0072:C3 06CC                    JP    06CCH             ; 06CC

0075:11 7880                    LD    DE,SUB_SDIV
0078:21 18F7                    LD    HL,_SUB_SDIV

start5a:
007B:01 0027                    LD    BC,0027H
007E:ED B0                      LDIR
0080:21 79E5                    LD    HL,line_buffer_mark
0083:36 3A                      LD    (HL),3AH          ; [79E5] {00}
0085:23                         INC   HL
0086:70                         LD    (HL),B            ; [79E6] {00}
0087:23                         INC   HL
0088:36 2C                      LD    (HL),2CH          ; [79E7] {00}
008A:23                         INC   HL

;******  ROM with basic found

00AC:31 79F8                    LD    SP,79F8H

00B2:CD 01C9                    CALL  BAS_CLS           ; 01C9
00B5:00                         NOP

00CF:77                         LD    (HL),A            ; [7B4D] {00}
00D0:BE                         CP    (HL)              ; [7B4D] {00}

00D2:28 F3                      JR    Z,00C7H           ; $-13 00C7

00E7:2B                         DEC   HL

;******  calc new pos (AF,DE)
;  I: -
;  O: A = new pos value
SUB_calc_pos:
0345:F1                         POP   AF
0346:D1                         POP   DE
0347:C9                         RET

0348:3A 783D                    LD    A,(KEY_old_7)     ; [783D] {00}
034B:E6 08                      AND   8
034D:3A 7820                    LD    A,(DCB_scr_pos)   ; [7820] {00}
0350:28 03                      JR    Z,0355H           ; $+3 0355
0352:0F                         RRCA
0353:E6 1F                      AND   1FH
0355:E6 1F                      AND   1FH
0357:C9                         RET

;******  get keyboard DOS (AF)
;  I: -
;  O: A = ASCII code of character
SUB_key_get_dos:
0358:CD 79C4                    CALL  DOS_key_running   ; 79C4
035B:D5                         PUSH  DE
035C:CD 002B                    CALL  KEY_get           ; 002B
035F:D1                         POP   DE
0360:C9                         RET

;******  input a line of max 240 characters (AF,DE,HL)
;  I: -
;  O: carry set if break pressed
SUB_input_line:
0361:0A                         LD    A,(BC)            ; [0000] {F3}
0362:0B                         DEC   BC
0363:0C                         INC   C
0364:0C                         INC   C
0365:0D                         DEC   C
0366:0E 0F                      LD    C,0FH
0368:0F                         RRCA
0369:10 11                      DJNZ  037CH             ; $+17 037C
036B:12                         LD    (DE),A            ; [0000] {F3}
036C:13                         INC   DE
036D:15                         DEC   D
